directory entry; ignore\n                continue\n            _write_member(name, info.compress_type)\n\n        # Write any files present on disk but not in original, last (shouldn't happen here).\n        for name, p in sorted(extracted_files.items()):\n            if name in written:\n                continue\n            info = zipfile.ZipInfo(filename=name)\n            info.compress_type = zipfile.ZIP_DEFLATED\n            info.date_time = (1980, 1, 1, 0, 0, 0)\n            zout.writestr(info, p.read_bytes())\n\n\n# -------------------------- main --------------------------\n\ndef main() -> int:\n    src_hwpx = Path(\"/root/renewal_playbook.hwpx\")\n    update_json_path = Path(\"/root/renewal_update.json\")\n    followups_path = Path(\"/root/followups.csv\")\n    out_hwpx = Path(\"/root/renewal_playbook_updated.hwpx\")\n\n    appendix_sentence = \"이 부록 문단은 그대로 유지해야 합니다.\"\n\n    update_json = json.loads(update_json_path.read_text(encoding=\"utf-8\"))\n    followups = read_followups_csv(followups_path)\n\n    if not followups:\n        raise RuntimeError(\"No follow-up rows found in followups.csv\")\n\n    replacements = build_replacements(update_json)\n\n    with tempfile.TemporaryDirectory(prefix=\"hwpx_update_\") as td:\n        td_path = Path(td)\n        extracted = td_path / \"extracted\"\n        extracted.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)\n\n        extract_hwpx(src_hwpx, extracted)\n\n        # If we inserted follow-up paragraphs, ensure they do not contain cached lineSegArray.\n            # (New paragraphs won't, but if the section mixes operations, be safe.)\n            if changed_fu:\n                for p in _iter_paragraphs(tree.getroot()):\n                    if _localname(p.tag) != \"p\":\n                        continue\n                    txt = _get_paragraph_text(p)\n                    if any(txt.strip().startswith(f\"{fu.sequence}.\") for fu in followups):\n                        _remove_lineseg_arrays_in_paragraph(p)\n\n            if changed_field or changed_fu:\n                tree.write(sf, encoding=\"utf-8\", xml_declaration=True)\n\n        repackage_hwpx(src_hwpx, extracted, out_hwpx)\n\n    print(f\"Wrote: {out_hwpx}\")\n    return 0\n\n\nif __name__ == \"__main__\":\n    try:\n        raise SystemExit(main())\n    except Exception as e:\n        print(f\"ERROR: {e}\", file=sys.stderr)\n        raise\n","structuredPatch":[],"originalFile":null,"userModified":false}}
